# Agents · S · part 35

1,120 entries, spec-writer to speckit.parallel-autonomous-status.agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **spec-writer**: Designs a feature's contract (behaviour, interfaces, data and state ownership, error mapping, write safety, security, observability) and writes the spec that gates implementation. Dispatch for any new feature, any interface decision, or when a spec is ambiguous. Use the reasoning tier for work with no precedent in the project. Does not write implementation code. ([ankeshshankar/agent-harness](https://github.com/ankeshshankar/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-writer.md) · 3 copies)
- **spec-writer**: Belirsiz bir görev isteğini kesin bir spesifikasyona dönüştürür. Kabul kriteri, kapsam dışı ve açık sorular üretir. Kullanıcı yeni bir özellik, değişiklik veya "şunu yapalım" dediğinde ve istek net değilse ilk adım olarak kullan. Kod yazmaz, sadece ne yapılacağını tanımlar. ([Ardaozhan/claudecalis](https://github.com/Ardaozhan/claudecalis/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-writer.md))
- **spec-writer**: Use when creating or updating specification documents in docs/spec/ or drafting GitHub spec issues. Triggers on requests like "仕様を書いて", "spec を更新", "新機能の仕様を整理", or any work that defines WHAT the app should do (rules, user flows, edge cases) before HOW it is implemented. Produces a complete spec document ready for review. ([argama147/OthelloJosekiMaster](https://github.com/argama147/OthelloJosekiMaster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-writer.md))
- **spec-writer**: Turn a vague idea into a spec another agent can build from, or reverse-engineer a spec from existing code. ([arhunsaday/agent-kit](https://github.com/arhunsaday/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/spec-writer.md))
- **spec-writer**: Creates detailed implementation specs on feature branches and pushes them (PR creation happens later via pr-creator) ([avays/ClaudeTools](https://github.com/avays/ClaudeTools/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-writer.md))

- **speckit.fx-to-dotnet.show-policy.agent**: Display a named migration policy document. - **speckit.fx-to-dotnet.specify-hook.agent**: after_specify hook (mandatory). Detect .NET Framework projects in the workspace and annotate spec.md with a Migration Context Detected section. Silent-exit success on non-Framework solutions. Idempotent. ([B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.fx-to-dotnet.specify-hook.agent.md))
- **speckit.fx-to-dotnet.tasks-hook.agent**: after_tasks hook (mandatory). Dedupe migration-themed tasks, insert a `## Phase 1: .NET Framework Migration` block ahead of all existing phases (renumbering them by +1), and emit granular [MIG-*] tasks each carrying a `dispatch: speckit.fx-to-dotnet.&lt;command&gt;(&lt;args&gt;)` trailer. Silent-exit on non-Framework solutions. Idempotent. ([B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.fx-to-dotnet.tasks-hook.agent.md))
- **speckit.fx-to-dotnet.update-packages.agent**: Execute a per-project package update plan; invoke build-fix after each (project, chunk) pair ([B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.fx-to-dotnet.update-packages.agent.md))
- **speckit.fx-to-dotnet.verify-hook.agent**: after_implement hook (optional). Silent-exit on non-Framework solutions. Idempotent. ([B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/OrthoPlus-Enterprise/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit.fx-to-dotnet.verify-hook.agent.md))
